# The unit tests, run in <repo>, a worktree of branch vmaf/phase-3, at the commit below with a clean working tree (docs/DECISIONS.md P-028, P-032, P-060, P-065, P-066, P-073, P-074, P-077, P-078): each command, then its output and exit status.
